# Host header extension inventory: generated appendix

Generated by `scripts/report.py` from the data described in [../host-header-extensions.md](../host-header-extensions.md) (section 0 and the method paragraph). Do not edit by hand; edit `scripts/appendix.md` and regenerate. Paths are shortened: `c++/` is `/usr/include/c++/15/`, `c++/aarch64/` is `/usr/include/aarch64-linux-gnu/c++/15/`, `aarch64/` is `/usr/include/aarch64-linux-gnu/`, `gcc/` is `/usr/lib/gcc/aarch64-linux-gnu/15/include/`, and anything else is relative to `/usr/include/`.

Column meanings are those of the main document: *Uses/files* counts every occurrence in the static closure; *Representative site* prefers an occurrence reached in M1 and outside a `#define`; *Innermost guard* is the nearest enclosing conditional other than an include guard, quoted from the header (an `#else` names the `#if` it belongs to); *Reached* is the reachability label; *Class* is M (semantics must be implemented), L (a call to the same-named library function is a correct implementation outside constant evaluation) or A (accepting and ignoring it preserves behaviour).
